# Independent relation-map review — round 8

Reviewed **`ec4eede`**, 2026-11-03 KST, against the supplied m8 design and the unchanged approval bar. **Approval is blocked by three reproduced originator-leak mechanisms.** The exact named m7 blockers are repaired, but `[89 Zr] trastuzumab` is a new regression; internal suffix wrappers and omitted Unicode dashes still evade the three-stage pipeline. Passing graph checks and demos do not establish that the selected identity is safe.

All new artifacts are under `audit_evidence_claims/m8/`; no tracked file was changed. Known **M5-S2** and explicitly intended same-item over-refusal are accepted. Loss of a valid drug alone in its item is reported separately, as requested.

## Verification and reproduction boundaries

- **334 passed in 33.83 s**, exit 0: `python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`. Bytecode disabled and temporary files confined to m8. [Test output](root_checks/pytest.txt), [environment and hashes](root_checks/pytest_summary.json).
- Earlier reproducers were **reused**, with output relocation/port changes documented. Ten inherited CLI HTTP runners completed successfully; the original m1 HTTP cases and seven QA demos are included. [Manifest](root_checks/reuse_manifest.json), [six runners](root_checks/execution_http.json), [four m6 runners](root_checks/execution_m6_http.json).
- The inherited name suite includes **13 scripts**, **six supplements**, **all five original m1 name scripts**, the **113-case / 7,511-key sweep**, and the frozen **1,648-question** no-relink comparison. One original alias sweep failed on a real application cache exception; the failure is preserved, and a separately documented catch-and-retry adaptation completed all inputs while recording the exception. This is not reported as an all-green replay. [Name replay report](replays/FINDINGS.md).
- The m7 attack script was rerun: **93 inputs, ten QA questions, 44 actual HTTP requests**. Its old `_whole_name` call first failed because the signature changed; the recorded mechanical adaptation supplies `found`, without changing inputs. Its old raw stage calls do not apply current NFKC normalization; current public-linker/map results determine dispositions. [Adaptation](m7_attacks/adaptation.json), [original failure](m7_attacks/signature_failure.txt), [results](m7_attacks/fresh_results.json), [HTTP](m7_attacks/http_results.json).
- Independent graph oracle: **6,469 live centres, 22 relation/direction/type/role combinations, zero stored-row mismatches**. Seven DB commands completed; **19/19 assertions** pass. Retired/malformed/numeric mutations use isolated in-memory fixtures, including the **1,001-Drug** count-chunk test. [Execution](db_ui/execution.json), [oracle](db_ui/db_validate.json), [assertions](db_ui/db_assertions.json).
- **36/36 complete page-demo responses equal m7**, all **36 demo callbacks plus six map callbacks** pass, and **24/24 UI assertions** pass. An original CLI server also returned all 36 demo responses identically over HTTP. [Demo/UI/cost report](demos_cost/FINDINGS.md).
- Fresh attacks: **174 distinct map inputs and eight QA questions**, exact prior-query-module comparison, **26 Unicode Pd characters**, and seven focused alias checks. Root independently confirmed **57 new HTTP requests** and **16 alias/control HTTP requests**, all HTTP 200 (including erroneous maps and refusals). [Stage/predecessor evidence](attacks/probe_results.json), [new HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [alias HTTP](alias_checks/new_http.json).
- UI scripts use Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They verify generated markup, callbacks, escaping and response ordering; they do **not** certify browser paint/layout or browser-executed XSS behavior. The original application served worktree-identical HTML over HTTP. No use of port 8765.

## Blocking findings

### M8-B1 — P1, new regression: the resolver refuses a tracer, but its refusal is lost

Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"[89 Zr] trastuzumab"}`.

Actual HTTP 200 draws **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585**, **13 trials**, `also:[]`. There is no separately accepted originator mention.

| Stage/control | Observed result |
|---|---|
| `/api/resolve` | `BLOCKED_CLASS_TERM`, `radiotracer_guard`, no drug ID; cleaned component is `trastuzumab` |
| `_resolver_blocked` after input normalization | `{}` |
| `_link` | Drug / CHEMBL1201585 |
| `_whole_name` | `None`; the unsafe item-linker result survives |
| Exact previous query module, `9bd7cb4` | Refused; no centre |
| `[89Zr] trastuzumab`, `89Zr trastuzumab` | Correctly refused |
| Bare `trastuzumab` | Correctly draws the unlabelled drug |

`Which trials evaluate [89 Zr] trastuzumab?` returns **12 originator evidence rows** over HTTP, first `NCT00016367|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201585|NCT00016367`. The bracket-without-space control returns zero. Sponsor QA independently returns 12 rows in the direct engine probe. Tab, newline and NBSP between the isotope mass and element reproduce the same new regression. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [exact predecessor/stages](attacks/probe_results.json), [detailed analysis](attacks/FINDINGS.md).

**Cause:** `src/query.py:187-190` re-tests the cleaned component instead of honoring the resolver's recorded `radiotracer_guard`. The resolver correctly checks the raw input (`src/normalize.py:738-746`), but its component has already lost the bracketed isotope. `_link` now strips the brackets from separate words (`src/query.py:239-242`), creating `89 Zr trastuzumab`. `_qualified` checks isotope labels only one word at a time (`112`); its multiword scan does not call `is_isotope_label` (`114-116`), although that predicate supports `89 Zr` (`src/textrules.py:289-298`). Aggressive alias matching still succeeds (`src/query.py:317-322`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`). The final whole-name tracer guard recognizes the original tracer and returns `None` (`src/query.py:211-212`), but that is treated only as absence of a whole-name match: it supplies no refusal and the caller returns the unsafe `found` result (`149-153`).

**Required outcome:** preserve the explicit tracer refusal and its affected molecule through cleaning and tokenization; this exact input must never display or answer from the unlabelled drug.

### M8-B2 — P1, inherited: internal suffix wrappers defeat every refusal stage

**`bevacizumab-(awwb)`** and **`bevacizumab-[awwb]`** each return HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Both also leaked in the exact predecessor. Controls `bevacizumab-awwb`, `(bevacizumab-awwb)` and spaced `bevacizumab - (awwb)` refuse.

The resolver accepts the plain molecule after parenthetical cleaning; `_resolver_blocked` is empty. The item linker leaves an interior opening bracket/parenthesis, defeating the anchored suffix guard, then removes the distinction in `canonical_key`. `_whole_name` returns `None`, leaving the leaked Drug. `awwb` is a suffix explicitly classified by the repository itself (`src/textrules.py:169-171`); no external pharmacology assumption is needed.

`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-(awwb)?` returns **12** originator rows over HTTP, first `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`. `bevacizumab-(awwb), osimertinib` still makes BEVACIZUMAB the centre and lists OSIMERTINIB as an alternative; the separate drug does not authorize the originator leak. [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages and analogous trastuzumab cases](attacks/probe_results.json).

**Cause:** edge-only wrapper removal at `src/query.py:239-242`, anchored guard at `src/textrules.py:156-157`, followed by broader positive canonicalization at `src/textrules.py:339-346` / `src/query.py:320-322`. Resolver cleaning is at `src/textrules.py:358-363`.

**Required outcome:** internal wrapper variants of a refused product must not gain originator identity through the lookup key.

### M8-B3 — P1, inherited: the advertised universal dash fold omits actual dash characters

**`bevacizumab⸺awwb`**, with **U+2E3A TWO-EM DASH**, returns HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, no refusal. Trial QA returns the same 12 originator rows. ASCII-hyphen and U+2011 controls refuse. The exact previous query module also leaks U+2E3A.

NFKC preserves this character. `_DASHES` at `src/query.py:100-101` excludes it; `_resolver_blocked` returns `{}`, `_link` accepts the aggressive alias key, and `_whole_name` returns `None`. The key erases the dash at `src/textrules.py:345`. A bounded scan of this Python runtime's **26 Pd code points** records **14 originator leaks**, including U+2E3B and U+2E40. This is an exhaustive Pd scan, not a claim to cover every dash-like Unicode symbol. [Inventory](attacks/unicode_dashes.json), [HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json), [stages](attacks/probe_results.json).

**Required outcome:** the normalization/refusal representation must cover the dash equivalences accepted by positive lookup. The source's “every Unicode dash” claim currently exceeds its implementation.

152: )
153: 
154: # "Bevacizumab-Pfizer", "Trastuzumab-dkst": a hyphenated sponsor name or an
155: # FDA four-letter suffix on an INN stem.
156: RE_BIOSIMILAR_SUFFIX = re.compile(
157:     r"^[A-Za-z]{6,}(?:mab|cept|tinib|ase|grastim|stim|poetin)\s*[\-–]\s*(?P<suffix>[A-Za-z]{2,12})$", re.I)
158: 
159: # Words that follow a drug name after a hyphen but are not suffixes at all.
160: # "pembrolizumab-free" is an absence, not a product.
161: SUFFIX_LOOKALIKES = {"free", "only", "like", "base", "type", "form", "dose",
162:                      "naive", "based", "alone", "mono", "plus", "arm"}
163: 
164: # Since 2017 the FDA gives a four-letter suffix to *originator* biologics as
165: # well (cemiplimab-rwlc, dostarlimab-gxly, tislelizumab-jsgr), so a suffix on
166: # its own does not mark a biosimilar. Treating every suffix as one blocked the
167: # originators. A four-letter lowercase suffix is a biosimilar only when it is a
168: # known biosimilar suffix; a longer or capitalised suffix is a sponsor name.
169: BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES = {
170:     # bevacizumab
171:     "awwb", "bvzr", "maly", "adcd", "tnjn", "nwgd",
172:     # trastuzumab
173:     "dkst", "pkrb", "dttb", "qyyp", "anns", "strf",
174:     # rituximab
175:     "abbs", "pvvr", "arrx",
176:     # pegfilgrastim / filgrastim
177:     "jmdb", "cbqv", "bmez", "apgf", "fpgk", "pbbk", "sndz", "aafi", "ayow",
178:     # denosumab
179:     "bbdz", "bmwo", "bnht", "dssb", "kyqq", "nxxp",
180: }
271: 
272: RE_PAREN = re.compile(r"\(([^()]*)\)|\[([^\[\]]*)\]")
273: 
274: # Development codes ("AZD9291", "BMS-986504", "PF-07224826", "TSC-204-A0201").
275: # One character apart is a different molecule, so these must never be matched
276: # approximately -- the usual string-similarity intuition is wrong here.
277: RE_DEV_CODE = re.compile(
278:     r"^[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{2,8}(?:[\s\-][A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$")
279: 
280: # Trailing lot/format suffixes that do not change the substance: "BMS-936558-01"
281: RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX = re.compile(r"^(?P<core>[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{3,8})[\s\-]0?\d{1,2}$")
282: RE_MULTISPACE = re.compile(r"\s+")
283: RE_PUNCT_EDGE = re.compile(r"^[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+|[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+$")
284: 
285: 
286: # Isotope labels on radiotracers: 18F, 68Ga, 99mTc, 177Lu, 64Cu, 89Zr, 131I.
287: # These look exactly like development codes but name an isotope, not a molecule,
288: # and the substance they label is usually not in a therapeutic vocabulary at all.
289: RE_ISOTOPE = re.compile(r"^\[?(\d{1,3}m?)[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?$")
290: ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS = {
291:     "f", "c", "n", "o", "ga", "cu", "zr", "i", "lu", "tc", "in", "y", "at",
292:     "ac", "pb", "re", "sm", "sr", "ra", "rb", "br", "h", "p", "s",
293: }
294: 
295: 
296: def is_isotope_label(text: str) -> bool:
297:     m = RE_ISOTOPE.match((text or "").strip())
298:     return bool(m and m.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
299:

338: 
339: def canonical_key(text: str) -> str:
340:     """Aggressive key used for dictionary lookup (never shown to a user)."""
341:     t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
342:     t = "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c))
343:     t = t.lower()
344:     t = t.replace("’", "'").replace("‐", "-").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
345:     t = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "", t)
346:     return t

+"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+PORT = 8899
+BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
+CASES = [
+    "bevacizumab_awwb", "bevacizumab__awwb", "bevacizumab-awwb",
+    "bevacizumab (awwb)", "bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab-(awwb)",
+    "[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab", "89-Zr trastuzumab",
+    "[89 Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab",
+    "bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib", "bevacizumab",
+    "Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate", "CHEMBL5315030",
+    "Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container", "CHEMBL1355736",
+    "40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin", "CHEMBL1908360",
+]
+
+def post(endpoint, text):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
+        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    with response:
+        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
+                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
+                "response": json.loads(response.read())}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
+db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
+before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
+with (OUT / "independent_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
+        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
+    results = []
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
+            try:
+                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
+        for name in CASES:
+            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
+                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
+            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
+                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
+        (OUT / "independent_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
+            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
+            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
+            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
+        (OUT / "independent_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
+            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
+            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
+            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
+        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

71:     def explore(self, text: str, per_group: int = 30) -> dict:
72:         """One live node and the live nodes its live edges tie it to, grouped by relation.
73: 
74:         `text` is a node id or a name the question linker reads as one entity. A name

76:         mapped to the originator. Each group keeps `per_group` neighbours, sorted as the
77:         page states: trials by start date (latest first), drugs by trials in the graph,
78:         sponsors by trials they lead, disease targets by association score. Sponsorship
79:         keeps its role (lead sponsor or collaborator)."""

|---|---|---|

| `42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)rapamycin` | EVEROLIMUS / CHEMBL1908360 | Refused. Same collapsed predicate; resolver also records a radiotracer guard after cleaning. |

These four are new should-fix regressions. Do not infer that every other isotope-bearing alias in the 29-name loss list is a defect; those require the explicit tracer/product policy. +## Blocking findings
+
+### M9-B1 - P1: positive name normalization remains broader than every refusal stage
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"bevacizumab_awwb"}`.
+
+Actual HTTP 200 draws **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials**, with no refusal. `bevacizumab__awwb` does the same. The ASCII-hyphen control `bevacizumab-awwb` is correctly refused. `bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib` still selects the forbidden BEVACIZUMAB centre; the separate valid drug does not authorize that identity.
+
+| Stage | `bevacizumab_awwb` |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | ALIAS to CHEMBL1201583, no refusal |
+| `_resolver_blocked(_fold(text))` | `{}` |
+| `_link(_fold(text))` | Drug CHEMBL1201583, no Refused entry |
+| `_whole_name(...)` | `None`, supplying no veto |
+| Public map / trial QA | Originator map / 12 originator evidence rows |
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab_awwb?` returns 12 originator rows over HTTP. The hyphen control returns zero. [Independent responses](root_checks/independent_http.json), [stage matrix](attacks/probe_results.json), [additional HTTP](attacks/http_results.json).
+
+**Cause:** the new punctuation fold at **`src/query.py:114-118`** uses `[^\w\s]+`; underscore is a word character in this regex, although Unicode classifies it as connector punctuation. It therefore remains unchanged. The suffix predicate is narrower (`src/textrules.py:156-157`), while the positive key **removes underscore** (`src/textrules.py:339-346`). The key retrieves the stored alias `Bevacizumab awwb`, and `_link` accepts it (`src/query.py:339-354`). Returning no whole-name match is not a refusal (`src/query.py:160-165`). `awwb` is explicitly a known biosimilar suffix in `src/textrules.py:169-173`.
+
+The same representation mismatch also admits **`bevácizumab-awwb`** (U+00E1, and its decomposed equivalent): the guard sees an accented letter outside its ASCII stem pattern, but `canonical_key` removes the accent and accepts the blocked name. Exact predecessor comparisons show these are **newly reported inherited holes**, not a claim that M8's exact repaired input regressed. Additional dotted-suffix variants are retained in the attack evidence, without claiming an exhaustive Unicode proof. [Predicate details](attacks/predicate_details.json), [predecessor comparison](attacks/predecessor_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** punctuation/character equivalences that recover a known refused product must not recover its originator through a more permissive positive key. Preserve the bare originator and separately listed valid-drug controls.
+
+### M9-B2 - P1: the punctuation-folded isotope is never checked as an isotope label
+
+**`[89.Zr] trastuzumab`** and **`89:Zr trastuzumab`** each return HTTP 200, **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 trials**, no refusal. Both trial questions return **12 originator evidence rows**. Controls `89-Zr trastuzumab`, `[89 Zr] trastuzumab` and `[89Zr] trastuzumab` refuse; bare `trastuzumab` works. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/independent_http.json), [all stages](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+After edge stripping (`src/query.py:257-260`), the item begins `89.Zr` or `89:Zr`. Its punctuation-folded form is `89-Zr trastuzumab`. `is_isotope_label("89-Zr")` is true under **`src/textrules.py:289-298`**, and directly passing those folded words through `_qualified` refuses them. But the folded variant at **`src/query.py:117-118`** reaches only `is_distinct_product` / `is_radiotracer`; **the one- and two-word isotope tests at `123-126` see only the original words**. The narrower full-tracer predicate does not recognize this unbracketed, space-separated form. Positive alias matching erases the punctuation and recovers the stored `89zr-trastuzumab` alias as the unlabelled molecule (`src/textrules.py:339-346`, `src/query.py:339-354`).
+
+The raw resolver, resolver veto and item linker all miss the refusal; whole-name fallback supplies no veto. This is an inherited input hole still present after M8, and violates the added requirement to run the item guards on the punctuation-folded item. [Predicate evidence](attacks/predicate_details.json), [predecessor evidence](attacks/predecessor_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** apply the complete item refusal policy, including isotope-label checks, to the permitted folded representation before accepting an originator identity.
+
+### M9-B3 - P1: a known suffix without a hyphen is accepted as the originator alias
+
+**`bevacizumab awwb`** and **`bevacizumab (awwb)`** return HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials**, with no refusal. Both trial questions return **12** originator rows. The exact repaired `bevacizumab-(awwb)` and ordinary `bevacizumab-awwb` controls refuse. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/independent_http.json).
+
+The stored alias `Bevacizumab awwb` itself points to the originator ID. The suffix policy recognizes `awwb` but only when the pattern includes a hyphen (`src/textrules.py:156-157,169-173`). Spaces are deliberately preserved by the new punctuation fold (`src/query.py:118`), while edge wrappers are stripped (`257-260`). Thus all refusal stages accept the dangerous lookup key; the whole-name shortcut can also return Drug for the exact spaced stored alias (`229-239`). A dictionary hit does not establish product identity when the same suffix is expressly rejected in the control spelling.
+
+This is a **newly reported inherited hole**, distinct from M8-B2's now-fixed interior-wrapper-with-hyphen reproduction. The current design's stored-name tracer exception does not authorize treating this known biosimilar suffix as the original molecule. [Stage and predecessor evidence](attacks/FINDINGS.md).
+
+**Required outcome:** known suffix product names must not draw originator edges merely because they are stored without a hyphen or surrounded by whitespace-separated wrappers.
+

+"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+PORT = 8899
+BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
+CASES = [
+    "bevacizumab_awwb", "bevacizumab__awwb", "bevacizumab-awwb",
+    "bevacizumab (awwb)", "bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab-(awwb)",
+    "[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab", "89-Zr trastuzumab",
+    "[89 Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab",
+    "bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib", "bevacizumab",
+    "Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate", "CHEMBL5315030",
+    "Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container", "CHEMBL1355736",
+    "40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin", "CHEMBL1908360",
+]
+
+def post(endpoint, text):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
+        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    with response:
+        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
+                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
+                "response": json.loads(response.read())}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
+db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
+before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
+with (OUT / "independent_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
+        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
+    results = []
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
+            try:
+                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
+        for name in CASES:
+            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
+                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
+            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
+                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
+        (OUT / "independent_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
+            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
+            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
+            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
+        (OUT / "independent_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
+            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
+            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
+            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
+        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

# Round 9 independent normalization/refusal attacks

Reviewed `3a362eb`, without changing tracked files. These are **newly reported inherited failures**, not new regressions introduced by this HEAD: a bounded comparison loads the exact `ec4eede:src/query.py` in memory and observes the same eight unsafe outputs. It also verifies the two control repairs, `bevacizumab-(awwb)` and `[89 Zr] trastuzumab`. [Predecessor evidence](predecessor_results.json).

The fresh matrix covers **146 distinct inputs and 16 QA questions**. Full records include raw and normalized resolver responses, `_resolver_blocked`, `_link`, `_whole_name`, final entities, map identity, and dictionary entries. Direct probes reopen SQLite using `mode=ro`. An independent original CLI server on owned port **8895** confirms **50 HTTP requests, all 200**; its served page matches `web/try.html` byte for byte, its process was stopped, and its port is closed. [Stages](probe_results.json), [compact matrix](probe_summary.json), [HTTP](http_results.json), [predicate details](predicate_details.json).

The first HTTP attempt used the wrong `question` key for QA; the application expects `text`. Those 12 controlled 400 responses are a **harness error**, retained in [http_wrong_payload.json](http_wrong_payload.json). The corrected rerun changes only that key and repeats the complete 50-request set. [Adaptation record](adaptation.json).

## M9-B1 — P1: the positive alias key removes distinctions the refusal guards retain

### Underscore punctuation

`POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"bevacizumab_awwb"}` returns **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583, 122 trials**, `also: []`. `/api/resolve` returns ALIAS of the same originator. `_resolver_blocked` is `{}`, `_link` emits the Drug, `_whole_name` is `None`, and no stage emits a refusal.

U+005F LOW LINE is Unicode category **Pc**, so it is punctuation, but the new punctuation fold at `src/query.py:115-118` uses `[^\w\s]+`. Python's `\w` includes underscore. Therefore the declared punctuation-to-hyphen view leaves `bevacizumab_awwb` unchanged. The anchored suffix guard requires a hyphen (`src/textrules.py:156-157`), whereas `canonical_key` drops the underscore (`src/textrules.py:339-346`) and finds the stored `Bevacizumab awwb` alias. Positive lookup is at `src/query.py:339-354`.

Controls `bevacizumab-awwb` and `bevacizumab-(awwb)` refuse. The suffix `awwb` is explicitly present in `BIOSIMILAR_FDA_SUFFIXES` at `src/textrules.py:169-171`; the identity conclusion is grounded in that repository rule and the stored alias, without an external pharmacology assumption. Measured variants `bevacizumab__awwb`, `bevacizumab-_awwb`, `bevacizumab_-awwb`, `bevacizumab_._awwb`, `bevacizumab _ awwb` and `trastuzumab_dkst` also leak. `rituximab_abbs` does not resolve, so that analogue is not counted as a leak.

### Accents and punctuated suffix letters

`bevácizumab-awwb` (U+00E1 in the molecule name), its decomposed equivalent `beva\u0301cizumab-awwb`, and `bevacizumab-áwwb` all draw the same originator with no refusal. The first two were confirmed over HTTP. NFKC folding at `src/query.py:105-106` preserves the composed accented letter. The suffix regex is ASCII-only, but the positive key uses NFKD and removes combining marks (`src/textrules.py:341-345`), so the accepted key becomes the canonical key of the forbidden suffix form. The raw resolver returns ALIAS rather than refusing. This is a shared guard/identity mismatch, not a lost existing resolver refusal.

The inherited m8-script cases `bevacizumab-a.w.w.b` and `bevacizumab-(a.w.w.b)` still draw BEVACIZUMAB. Punctuation folding produces internal hyphens in the suffix, which the anchored `[A-Za-z]{2,12}` guard does not accept; the lookup key then removes them. These are supplementary reproductions of the same broader positive-key mismatch, not a claim that m8's exact simple-wrapper blocker remains unfixed.

For both `bevacizumab_awwb` and `bevácizumab-awwb`, HTTP trial QA and sponsor QA each return **12 originator evidence rows**. The first trial edge is `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`; the first sponsor edge is `ORG:roche|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL1201583|`. The ordinary hyphen control yields zero for both QA forms.

Required outcome: a representation accepted as the known suffix-bearing alias must not acquire originator identity merely because its punctuation or accent is dropped only on the positive-lookup path. Apply the stated punctuation treatment consistently and check the equivalences the positive key actually accepts.

## M9-B2 — P1: the punctuation-folded view does not reach the isotope-label guards

`POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"[89.Zr] trastuzumab"}` returns **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585, 13 trials**, with no refusal. The raw resolver extracts the parenthetical and returns EXACT trastuzumab; `_resolver_blocked` is `{}`; the item linker returns the Drug via the `89zr-trastuzumab` alias; `_whole_name` is `None`.

The item token after edge-wrapper removal is `89.Zr`. The new punctuation fold converts it to `89-Zr`, and **`T.is_isotope_label('89-Zr')` is true**. But `hit` at `src/query.py:114-118` applies only `is_distinct_product` and `is_radiotracer` to the folded view. The one- and two-word isotope-label tests at `src/query.py:122-126` see only the original tokens. `is_radiotracer('89-Zr trastuzumab')` is false: its anchored grammar expects an additional hyphen between isotope and carrier or a bracketed isotope (`src/textrules.py:303-314`). This leaves the declared folded-word isotope check unperformed before aggressive alias matching.

`89:Zr trastuzumab`, `89.Zr trastuzumab`, `[89:Zr] trastuzumab` and `89::Zr trastuzumab` reproduce the same mechanism. `89-Zr trastuzumab`, `89 Zr trastuzumab`, `[89 Zr] trastuzumab` and `[89Zr] trastuzumab` correctly refuse. `89.Zr-trastuzumab` also refuses because its folded complete string satisfies the separate tracer grammar. These controls isolate the missing application of the isotope-label predicate; the report does not assert that any arbitrary unfamiliar isotope spelling is a valid medical name.

HTTP trial and sponsor QA for `[89.Zr] trastuzumab` each return **12 originator evidence rows**. The first trial edge is `NCT00016367|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201585|NCT00016367`; the first sponsor edge is `ORG:nationalcancerinstitute|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL1201585|`. Both QA forms for the hyphenated-token control `89-Zr trastuzumab` return zero.

Required outcome: run the one- and two-word isotope-label tests on the punctuation-folded item representation as well, preserving the specified lone-stored-name exception.

## M9-B3 — P1: a known biosimilar suffix separated by whitespace is accepted as an exact originator alias

`bevacizumab awwb`, `bevacizumab (awwb)` and `bevacizumab [awwb]` all draw **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583, 122 trials**, without refusal. The first two are independently confirmed over HTTP. The repository itself records `awwb` as a biosimilar suffix (`src/textrules.py:169-171`), while the dictionary also lists `Bevacizumab awwb` under the originator. The whole-name exception is not permission to erase that product distinction: the user grants that exception only to the tracer rule for a lone stored name, and the code's whole-name docstring explicitly says product refusals must win (`src/query.py:221-226`).

The guard requires a hyphen at `src/textrules.py:156-157`; whitespace alone is not accepted. The raw resolver returns ALIAS for `bevacizumab awwb`, or EXACT plain bevacizumab after extracting `(awwb)` / `[awwb]` (`src/textrules.py:358-363`). Stage one thus has no recorded refusal. Stage two strips the separate word's edge wrappers at `src/query.py:257-260`, leaving `bevacizumab awwb`; neither raw nor punctuation-folded guard view supplies a hyphen. It resolves the complete alias. Stage three returns the originator for the literal stored alias, or `None` for the wrapped forms, leaving the unsafe positive result intact (`src/query.py:229-239`).

`trastuzumab dkst` and `trastuzumab (dkst)` likewise draw TRASTUZUMAB. Controls with the explicit hyphen refuse. This is distinct from M9-B1's punctuation mismatch: even the literal stored alias has the problem. Both HTTP QA forms for `bevacizumab (awwb)` return 12 originator rows.

Required outcome: recognize the repository's known biosimilar suffix when whitespace or a separate wrapper separates it from the named originator, before accepting the alias. A separately named legitimate drug should continue to survive according to the stated item policy.

## Passing boundaries and limits

All **16 fresh comma/semicolon/slash/pipe and parenthesis carry-boundary cases** retained the separately listed osimertinib. This includes `biosimilar (and), osimertinib`, `biosimilar (and)/osimertinib`, `biosimilar, (and osimertinib)`, and `biosimilar and (/ osimertinib)`. The repaired `biosimilar, and osimertinib`, `biosimilar; and osimertinib`, and `biosimilar and, osimertinib` controls also pass.

The direct controls `566C`, `9-ac`, `5ac` and their explicitly delimited combinations retain their stored identities; no new lone-name loss is claimed from this subset. The historical sweep worker owns the full alias audit. `Pegylated interferon alfa-2a` remains the explicitly accepted M5-S2 refusal. Basic valid molecules and legitimate originator FDA suffixes retain their identities.

All implicated positive maps use real live graph rows; the defect is the selected identity, not fabricated graph edges. These are sufficient originator blockers even though existing demos may pass. Root/sibling workers own historical disposition matrices, exhaustive DB/UI checks, demos and costs.

+"""Verify original tracked bytes and review artifacts; never mutate the application."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import datetime
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import socket
+import subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+before = json.loads((OUT / 'root_checks/integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+
+def git(*args):
+    got = subprocess.run(['git', *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
+    return {'code': got.returncode, 'stdout': got.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace'),
+            'stderr': got.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+
+changed = []
+missing = []
+for rel, digest in before['files'].items():
+    file = ROOT / rel
+    if not file.exists():
+        missing.append(rel)
+    elif hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
+        changed.append(rel)
+tracked_now = set(git('ls-files', '-z')['stdout'].split('\0')) - {''}
+ports = {}
+for port in [8891, 8892, 8893, 8894, 8895, 8897, 8898, 8899, 8901]:
+    with socket.socket() as sock:
+        sock.settimeout(.2)
+        ports[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
+report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+links = re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', report)
+broken = [s for s in links if s != 'integrity_final.json' and not (OUT / s).exists()]
+db_hash = hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+result = {
+    'time': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
+    'head_before': before['head'], 'head_after': git('rev-parse', 'HEAD')['stdout'].strip(),
+    'tracked_files_hashed': len(before['files']), 'changed_tracked_files': changed,
+    'missing_tracked_files': missing, 'new_tracked_paths': sorted(tracked_now-set(before['files'])),
+    'tracked_diff': git('diff', '--exit-code'), 'staged_diff': git('diff', '--cached', '--exit-code'),
+    'git_status': git('status', '--short'),
+    'database_sha256_before': before['db_sha256'], 'database_sha256_after': db_hash,
+    'database_unchanged': before['db_sha256'] == db_hash,
+    'owned_ports_closed': ports, 'report_links_checked': len(links),
+    'broken_report_links': broken,
+    'report_final_line': report.rstrip().splitlines()[-1],
+    'report_sha256': hashlib.sha256((OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+}
+result['passed'] = (not changed and not missing and not result['new_tracked_paths']
+    and result['head_before'] == result['head_after']
+    and result['tracked_diff']['code'] == result['staged_diff']['code'] == 0
+    and result['database_unchanged'] and all(ports.values()) and not broken
+    and result['report_final_line'] == 'VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED')
+(OUT / 'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
+assert result['passed'], 'Final integrity verification failed; inspect JSON'

+## Should-fix findings, separate from originator blockers
+
+### M9-S1 - P2, new regressions: four exact standalone stored aliases are now refused
+
+| Exact input | Correct stored drug | Current HTTP result |
+|---|---|---|
+| `Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate` | TRASTUZUMAB VEDOTIN / CHEMBL5315030 | 200, Refused, no centre |
+| `Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container` | THEOPHYLLINE / CHEMBL1355736 | 200, Refused, no centre |
+| `40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin` | EVEROLIMUS / CHEMBL1908360 | 200, Refused, no centre |
+| `42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)rapamycin` | EVEROLIMUS / CHEMBL1908360 | 200, Refused, no centre |
+
+Each is an exact unique dictionary alias supplied alone. The exact M8 query module (`ec4eede`), loaded with the same app/rules/data, draws its correct live Drug; preferred-name/ID controls still work. The exact old set of **19 non-M5-S2 lost aliases remains repaired**; these are four other inputs. [Stored identities, old/current stages and ten HTTP requests](names/new_alias_confirmation.json), [independent confirmation of the first three](root_checks/independent_http.json), [old 19 check](names/historical_alias_set_check.json).
+
+For the ADC, punctuation collapse turns the truncated word window into `Trastuzumab-vcmmae`, firing the generic suffix guard. For the chemical-locant names, collapse creates a tracer-like prefix. For theophylline, edge stripping removes `%` (`src/query.py:257-260`), and the new adjacent-word isotope rule reads **`5 in`** as mass/element (`125-126`). All four complete raw spellings have false raw distinct-product/radiotracer predicates; the new item checks create the refusal. Whole-name recovery then exits because `found` contains Refused (`227-228`). The resolver exception at `196` covers only pieces tokenized as one word, not these complete stored names.
+
+These losses fall outside the user's intended over-refusal allowance because the valid named drug is alone in its item. Preserve its stored ID without weakening the product/tracer controls. The larger alias sweep contains additional isotope/product-bearing names; they are retained in raw evidence but not all asserted to be defects. M5-S2 remains the accepted known exception.

+"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+PORT = 8899
+BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
+CASES = [
+    "bevacizumab_awwb", "bevacizumab__awwb", "bevacizumab-awwb",
+    "bevacizumab (awwb)", "bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab-(awwb)",
+    "[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab", "89-Zr trastuzumab",
+    "[89 Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab",
+    "bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib", "bevacizumab",
+    "Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate", "CHEMBL5315030",
+    "Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container", "CHEMBL1355736",
+    "40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin", "CHEMBL1908360",
+]
+
+def post(endpoint, text):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
+        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    with response:
+        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
+                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
+                "response": json.loads(response.read())}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
+db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
+before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
+with (OUT / "independent_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
+        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
+    results = []
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
+            try:
+                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
+        for name in CASES:
+            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
+                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
+            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
+                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
+        (OUT / "independent_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
+            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
+            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
+            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
+        (OUT / "independent_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
+            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
+            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
+            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
+        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

+"""Verify original tracked bytes and review artifacts; never mutate the application."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import datetime
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import socket
+import subprocess
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+before = json.loads((OUT / 'root_checks/integrity_before.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+
+def git(*args):
+    got = subprocess.run(['git', *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
+    return {'code': got.returncode, 'stdout': got.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace'),
+            'stderr': got.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
+
+changed = []
+missing = []
+for rel, digest in before['files'].items():
+    file = ROOT / rel
+    if not file.exists():
+        missing.append(rel)
+    elif hashlib.sha256(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
+        changed.append(rel)
+tracked_now = set(git('ls-files', '-z')['stdout'].split('\0')) - {''}
+ports = {}
+for port in [8891, 8892, 8893, 8894, 8895, 8897, 8898, 8899, 8901]:
+    with socket.socket() as sock:
+        sock.settimeout(.2)
+        ports[str(port)] = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', port)) != 0
+report = (OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+links = re.findall(r'\]\(([^)]+)\)', report)
+broken = [s for s in links if s != 'integrity_final.json' and not (OUT / s).exists()]
+db_hash = hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+result = {
+    'time': datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
+    'head_before': before['head'], 'head_after': git('rev-parse', 'HEAD')['stdout'].strip(),
+    'tracked_files_hashed': len(before['files']), 'changed_tracked_files': changed,
+    'missing_tracked_files': missing, 'new_tracked_paths': sorted(tracked_now-set(before['files'])),
+    'tracked_diff': git('diff', '--exit-code'), 'staged_diff': git('diff', '--cached', '--exit-code'),
+    'git_status': git('status', '--short'),
+    'database_sha256_before': before['db_sha256'], 'database_sha256_after': db_hash,
+    'database_unchanged': before['db_sha256'] == db_hash,
+    'owned_ports_closed': ports, 'report_links_checked': len(links),
+    'broken_report_links': broken,
+    'report_final_line': report.rstrip().splitlines()[-1],
+    'report_sha256': hashlib.sha256((OUT / 'REPORT.md').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+}
+result['passed'] = (not changed and not missing and not result['new_tracked_paths']
+    and result['head_before'] == result['head_after']
+    and result['tracked_diff']['code'] == result['staged_diff']['code'] == 0
+    and result['database_unchanged'] and all(ports.values()) and not broken
+    and result['report_final_line'] == 'VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED')
+(OUT / 'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
+assert result['passed'], 'Final integrity verification failed; inspect JSON'

+
+Reviewed **`3a362eb4f156ea252301d18507ff0a733d0b601e`**, 2026-11-03 KST, against the supplied round-9 design and original approval bar. **Approval is blocked by reproducible originator leaks that every refusal stage misses.** All seven named M8 findings are fixed for their exact inputs. +## Verification and limits
+
+- **336 passed in 23.63 s**, exit 0: `python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`, with bytecode disabled and temporary files under m9. [Output](root_checks/pytest.txt), [environment/hashes](root_checks/pytest_summary.json).
+- Reused all earlier finding reproducers, relocating outputs and owned ports. Root's six inherited HTTP runners and four M6 runners all exit 0; the original M1 HTTP cases, seven original QA demos and M8's 57-request confirmation are included. [Reuse manifest](root_checks/reuse_manifest.json), [six runners](root_checks/execution_http.json), [four runners](root_checks/execution_m6_http.json), [M8 HTTP](root_checks/new_http.json).

The literal old alias sweep now finishes without the old cache crash. Known obsolete harness calls use the documented M8 adaptations; inert old monkey-patches are not used as evidence for current no-relink behavior. [Name report](names/FINDINGS.md), [manifest](names/reuse_manifest.json), [execution](names/round9_execution.json).

Reused the M8 attack script: **174 inputs / 8 QA**, all **26 Unicode Pd** characters, plus **16 alias/control HTTP requests**. [M7](names/m7_attacks/fresh_results.json), [M8](names/attacks/probe_results.json), [aliases](names/alias_checks/new_http.json).
+- Independent graph oracle: **6,469 live centres, 22 relation/direction/type/role combinations, zero errors**. **19/19 DB assertions** pass, including live trial counts, retired endpoints, malformed props, unknown counts, exact score precision and the 1,001-drug chunking fixture. [Oracle](db_ui_cost/db_validate.json), [assertions](db_ui_cost/db_assertions.json).

Records include resolver output, normalized input, resolver veto, item linker, whole-name result, public linking and map output. An initial wrong QA payload key was a harness error, preserved and corrected before the full rerun; it is not an application finding. Root independently confirmed **54 additional actual HTTP responses** with the original CLI server. [Fresh attacks](attacks/FINDINGS.md), [adaptation](attacks/adaptation.json), [independent HTTP](root_checks/independent_http.json), [lifecycle](root_checks/independent_lifecycle.json).
+- UI checks execute actual page JavaScript in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. They verify generated markup, callbacks and stale-response behavior, not browser paint or real-browser exploitation. Served HTML was byte-identical to the worktree. Port 8765 was not used. No clinical interpretation or external pharmacology assumption is needed for the findings below: the repository's own suffix/isotope rules and stored identities supply the controls.
+
+## Blocking findings
+
+### M9-B1 - P1: positive name normalization remains broader than every refusal stage
+
+Exact request: `POST /api/explore` with `{"text":"bevacizumab_awwb"}`.
+
+Actual HTTP 200 draws **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials**, with no refusal. `bevacizumab__awwb` does the same. The ASCII-hyphen control `bevacizumab-awwb` is correctly refused. `bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib` still selects the forbidden BEVACIZUMAB centre; the separate valid drug does not authorize that identity.
+
+| Stage | `bevacizumab_awwb` |
+|---|---|
+| Raw resolver | ALIAS to CHEMBL1201583, no refusal |
+| `_resolver_blocked(_fold(text))` | `{}` |
+| `_link(_fold(text))` | Drug CHEMBL1201583, no Refused entry |
+| `_whole_name(...)` | `None`, supplying no veto |
+| Public map / trial QA | Originator map / 12 originator evidence rows |
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab_awwb?` returns 12 originator rows over HTTP. The hyphen control returns zero. [Independent responses](root_checks/independent_http.json), [stage matrix](attacks/probe_results.json), [additional HTTP](attacks/http_results.json).
+
+**Cause:** the new punctuation fold at **`src/query.py:114-118`** uses `[^\w\s]+`; underscore is a word character in this regex, although Unicode classifies it as connector punctuation. It therefore remains unchanged. The suffix predicate is narrower (`src/textrules.py:156-157`), while the positive key **removes underscore** (`src/textrules.py:339-346`). The key retrieves the stored alias `Bevacizumab awwb`, and `_link` accepts it (`src/query.py:339-354`). Returning no whole-name match is not a refusal (`src/query.py:160-165`). `awwb` is explicitly a known biosimilar suffix in `src/textrules.py:169-173`.
+
+The same representation mismatch also admits **`bevácizumab-awwb`** (U+00E1, and its decomposed equivalent): the guard sees an accented letter outside its ASCII stem pattern, but `canonical_key` removes the accent and accepts the blocked name. Exact predecessor comparisons show these are **newly reported inherited holes**, not a claim that M8's exact repaired input regressed. Additional dotted-suffix variants are retained in the attack evidence, without claiming an exhaustive Unicode proof. [Predicate details](attacks/predicate_details.json), [predecessor comparison](attacks/predecessor_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** punctuation/character equivalences that recover a known refused product must not recover its originator through a more permissive positive key. Preserve the bare originator and separately listed valid-drug controls.
+
+### M9-B2 - P1: the punctuation-folded isotope is never checked as an isotope label
+
+**`[89.Zr] trastuzumab`** and **`89:Zr trastuzumab`** each return HTTP 200, **TRASTUZUMAB / CHEMBL1201585 / 13 trials**, no refusal. Both trial questions return **12 originator evidence rows**. Controls `89-Zr trastuzumab`, `[89 Zr] trastuzumab` and `[89Zr] trastuzumab` refuse; bare `trastuzumab` works. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/independent_http.json), [all stages](attacks/probe_results.json).
+
+After edge stripping (`src/query.py:257-260`), the item begins `89.Zr` or `89:Zr`. Its punctuation-folded form is `89-Zr trastuzumab`. `is_isotope_label("89-Zr")` is true under **`src/textrules.py:289-298`**, and the literal folded control is refused. But the folded variant at **`src/query.py:117-118`** reaches only `is_distinct_product` / `is_radiotracer`; **the one- and two-word isotope tests at `123-126` see only the original words**. The narrower full-tracer predicate does not recognize this unbracketed, space-separated form. Positive alias matching erases the punctuation and recovers the stored `89zr-trastuzumab` alias as the unlabelled molecule (`src/textrules.py:339-346`, `src/query.py:339-354`).
+
+The raw resolver, resolver veto and item linker all miss the refusal; whole-name fallback supplies no veto. This is an inherited input hole still present after M8, and violates the added requirement to run the item guards on the punctuation-folded item. [Predicate evidence](attacks/predicate_details.json), [predecessor evidence](attacks/predecessor_results.json).
+
+**Required outcome:** apply the complete item refusal policy, including isotope-label checks, to the permitted folded representation before accepting an originator identity.
+
+### M9-B3 - P1: a known suffix without a hyphen is accepted as the originator alias
+
+**`bevacizumab awwb`** and **`bevacizumab (awwb)`** return HTTP 200, **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 / 122 trials**, with no refusal. Both trial questions return **12** originator rows. The exact repaired `bevacizumab-(awwb)` and ordinary `bevacizumab-awwb` controls refuse. [Independent HTTP](root_checks/independent_http.json).
+
+The stored alias `Bevacizumab awwb` itself points to the originator ID. The suffix policy recognizes `awwb` but only when the pattern includes a hyphen (`src/textrules.py:156-157,169-173`). Spaces are deliberately preserved by the new punctuation fold (`src/query.py:118`), while edge wrappers are stripped (`257-260`). Thus all refusal stages miss the product distinction; the whole-name shortcut can also return Drug for the exact spaced stored alias (`229-239`). A dictionary hit does not establish product identity when the same suffix is expressly rejected in the control spelling.
+
+This is a **newly reported inherited hole**, distinct from M8-B2's now-fixed interior-wrapper-with-hyphen reproduction. The current design's stored-name tracer exception does not authorize treating this known biosimilar suffix as the original molecule. [Stage and predecessor evidence](attacks/FINDINGS.md).
+
+**Required outcome:** known suffix product names must not draw originator edges merely because they are stored without a hyphen or surrounded by whitespace-separated wrappers.

+"""Independent CLI HTTP confirmation of candidate round-nine findings."""
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import socket
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[2]
+PORT = 8899
+BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
+CASES = [
+    "bevacizumab_awwb", "bevacizumab__awwb", "bevacizumab-awwb",
+    "bevacizumab (awwb)", "bevacizumab awwb", "bevacizumab-(awwb)",
+    "[89.Zr] trastuzumab", "89:Zr trastuzumab", "89-Zr trastuzumab",
+    "[89 Zr] trastuzumab", "trastuzumab",
+    "bevacizumab_awwb, osimertinib", "bevacizumab",
+    "Trastuzumab--vcmmae conjugate", "CHEMBL5315030",
+    "Theophylline in dextrose 5% in plastic container", "CHEMBL1355736",
+    "40-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-rapamycin", "CHEMBL1908360",
+]
+
+def post(endpoint, text):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + "/api/" + endpoint,
+        data=json.dumps({"text": text}).encode(),
+        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+        response = error
+    with response:
+        return {"endpoint": endpoint, "input": text, "status": response.status,
+                "seconds": time.perf_counter() - start,
+                "response": json.loads(response.read())}
+
+with socket.socket() as sock:
+    assert sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "owned port already occupied"
+db = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"
+before = hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"}
+with (OUT / "independent_server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
+    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)],
+        cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
+        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
+    results = []
+    try:
+        for _ in range(100):
+            assert proc.poll() is None, "server exited"
+            try:
+                page = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
+                break
+            except OSError:
+                time.sleep(.1)
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
+        for name in CASES:
+            for endpoint in ("resolve", "explore"):
+                results.append(post(endpoint, name))
+            if not name.startswith("CHEMBL"):
+                results.append(post("ask", f"Which trials evaluate {name}?"))
+        (OUT / "independent_http.json").write_text(json.dumps(results,
+            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        print(json.dumps([{"input": r["input"], "status": r["status"],
+            "center": r["response"].get("center", {}).get("id"),
+            "refused": r["response"].get("refused")}
+            for r in results if r["endpoint"] == "explore"], indent=2))
+    finally:
+        proc.terminate()
+        proc.wait(timeout=10)
+        with socket.socket() as sock:
+            closed = sock.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
+        (OUT / "independent_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+            "pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT, "port_closed": closed,
+            "database_unchanged": hashlib.sha256(db.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == before,
+            "requests": len(results), "all_200": all(r["status"] == 200 for r in results),
+            "page_matches_worktree": page == (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_bytes()
+        }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
